# Agents · B · part 12

963 entries, bq-cost-analyzer to brand-qa. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **bq-cost-analyzer**: Use when asked to analyze BigQuery SQL files across a project for cost optimization opportunities, estimate total query costs, or audit a codebase for expensive query patterns. &lt;example&gt;Analyze all my SQL files for cost optimization opportunities&lt;/example&gt; &lt;example&gt;Which queries in this project are the most expensive?&lt;/example&gt; &lt;example&gt;Audit my BigQuery queries for cost reduction&lt;/example&gt; ([justvinhhere/bigquery-expert](https://github.com/justvinhhere/bigquery-expert/blob/HEAD/agents/bq-cost-analyzer.md) · ★17 · 2 copies)
- **bq-data-agent**: BigQuery Data & Metadata Engineer sub-agent for Cymbal Bank. Handles Task 1.1 (creating the cymbal_compliance dataset in US and ingesting 5 CSVs from GCS) and Task 1.3 (populating table and column descriptions across all 5 tables). Invoke in Wave 1. ([mbychkowski/capstone-plugin](https://github.com/mbychkowski/capstone-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/bq-data-agent.md))
- **bq-explore**: Isolated BigQuery exploration. Use whenever a question needs schema hunting, param discovery, or query iteration against GA4 data - anything that would otherwise fill the main session with table dumps and failed attempts. Returns a validated query plus the numbers it produced, not the search that found them. Give it the business question and any known constraints. ([0trm/analytics-loop](https://github.com/0trm/analytics-loop/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/bq-explore.md))
- **bq-red-team**: Use on a plan, a design or a diff that is risky (security, money, data, anything that runs code on users' machines, auth, production) or before a release, to attack it from outside the builder's assumptions. Returns ranked findings with a concrete failure scenario each. Read-only. ([xpShawky/BeQuite](https://github.com/xpShawky/BeQuite/blob/HEAD/agents/bq-red-team.md) · ★3)
- **bq-researcher**: Use when a decision needs reading wider than five sources (a stack or tool choice, a platform rule, a price, a version, a competitor sweep), so the reading stays out of the main context. Returns dated, cited findings and names every gap as UNVERIFIED. Read-only. ([xpShawky/BeQuite](https://github.com/xpShawky/BeQuite/blob/HEAD/agents/bq-researcher.md) · ★3)
- **bq-reviewer**: Use when asked to review all SQL files in a project or directory for BigQuery anti-patterns, scan a codebase for SQL performance issues, or audit BigQuery queries across multiple files. &lt;example&gt;Scan all SQL files in this project for BigQuery anti-patterns&lt;/example&gt; &lt;example&gt;Audit my BigQuery queries for performance issues&lt;/example&gt; &lt;example&gt;Review all the SQL in this repo and tell me what to optimize&lt;/example&gt; ([justvinhhere/bigquery-expert](https://github.com/justvinhhere/bigquery-expert/blob/HEAD/agents/bq-reviewer.md) · ★17 · 2 copies)
- **bq-schema-advisor**: Use when asked to analyze table schemas across a project, recommend partitioning and clustering strategies for existing tables, audit schema design, or optimize table structure across a dataset or project. &lt;example&gt;Analyze my BigQuery schemas and recommend partitioning strategies&lt;/example&gt; &lt;example&gt;Audit the table designs in this project and suggest improvements&lt;/example&gt; &lt;example&gt;Review my DDL files and optimize the schema design&lt;/example&gt; ([justvinhhere/bigquery-expert](https://github.com/justvinhhere/bigquery-expert/blob/HEAD/agents/bq-schema-advisor.md) · ★17 · 2 copies)
- **bq-scout**: Use for a quick, cheap sweep of what is new or moving (releases, changelogs, trending repos, a niche's recent videos or posts) when the answer is a short list of links with numbers, not a judgment. Runs on the fast model. Read-only. ([xpShawky/BeQuite](https://github.com/xpShawky/BeQuite/blob/HEAD/agents/bq-scout.md) · ★3)
- **bq-specialist**: when analyzing with sql on bigquery platform ([bongho/claude-env-sync](https://github.com/bongho/claude-env-sync/blob/HEAD/agents/bq-specialist.md))
- **bq-verifier**: Use before any "done", release or hand-off claim, so the check is independent of the agent that built the work. Re-runs the evidence (the commands in evidence.md, the test suite, the self-check) and returns pass or fail with the real output. Never edits files. ([xpShawky/BeQuite](https://github.com/xpShawky/BeQuite/blob/HEAD/agents/bq-verifier.md) · ★3)
- **br-backend-developer**: Implement a bounded NestJS API task with thin controllers, Swagger decorators and Prisma/service boundaries. Include focused tests. Use when separate backend specialization is useful. ([Lordpluha/bitrate](https://github.com/Lordpluha/bitrate/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/br-backend-developer.md) · ★4)
- **br-debugger**: Investigate a difficult unknown root cause, reproduce it, apply a surgical fix and verify the reproduction. Use Opus reasoning for complex diagnosis rather than every bug-shaped task. ([Lordpluha/bitrate](https://github.com/Lordpluha/bitrate/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/br-debugger.md) · ★4)
- **br-desktop-developer**: Implement a bounded Tauri + React task in apps/desktop. Preserve Rust/JS contracts, capabilities and CSP boundaries. Include focused tests. ([Lordpluha/bitrate](https://github.com/Lordpluha/bitrate/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/br-desktop-developer.md) · ★4)
- **br-developer**: BMAD-Ralph Developer Agent — Implements stories autonomously following architecture specs ([Franlao/bmad-ralph-skill](https://github.com/Franlao/bmad-ralph-skill/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/br-developer.md))
- **br-devops**: Implement a bounded CI, infrastructure or release change. Validate affected configuration and review permissions, secrets and injection risks; escalate difficult risks for independent review. ([Lordpluha/bitrate](https://github.com/Lordpluha/bitrate/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/br-devops.md) · ★4)
- **br-frontend-developer**: Implement a bounded task in web-player, web-artists, ui-react or the Svelte player. Follow scoped framework rules, reuse existing UI and write focused tests. Use when separate frontend specialization is useful. ([Lordpluha/bitrate](https://github.com/Lordpluha/bitrate/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/br-frontend-developer.md) · ★4)
- **br-librarian**: Audit a broad documentation scope independently when requested or justified. Report inconsistencies and proposed fixes without editing files or rewriting historical ADR decisions. ([Lordpluha/bitrate](https://github.com/Lordpluha/bitrate/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/br-librarian.md) · ★4)
- **br-manager**: Coordinate an explicitly requested interactive tracker workflow: issues, PRs and board state. Confirm each GitHub mutation. Never run under /br-auto or act as a mandatory preflight for ordinary code edits. ([Lordpluha/bitrate](https://github.com/Lordpluha/bitrate/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/br-manager.md) · ★4)
- **br-mobile-developer**: Implement a bounded React Native + Expo task in apps/mobile. Preserve native behavior and identify unestablished conventions. Include focused tests. ([Lordpluha/bitrate](https://github.com/Lordpluha/bitrate/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/br-mobile-developer.md) · ★4)
- **br-planner**: Plan a complex or cross-cutting task when a separate planning pass is useful. Return ordered steps, dependencies and open decisions; do not implement. ([Lordpluha/bitrate](https://github.com/Lordpluha/bitrate/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/br-planner.md) · ★4)
- **br-pre-scorer**: Generate LLM-suggested Business Relevance scores (1-5) plus a one-line rationale per TIP candidate in a value-modeler project, so the user starts Phase 3 scoring as an editor instead of an author. High-volume rubric scoring — runs once per project before the scoring UI is rendered. ([cogni-work/insight-wave](https://github.com/cogni-work/insight-wave/blob/HEAD/cogni-trends/agents/br-pre-scorer.md) · ★15)
- **br-qa**: BMAD-Ralph QA Agent — Reviews code for quality, security, and architecture compliance ([Franlao/bmad-ralph-skill](https://github.com/Franlao/bmad-ralph-skill/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/br-qa.md))
- **br-reviewer**: Perform requested independent or materially risky code review with evidence and a PASS/PARTIAL/FAIL verdict. Use relevant checklist sections; do not edit code or trigger solely on diff size. ([Lordpluha/bitrate](https://github.com/Lordpluha/bitrate/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/br-reviewer.md) · ★4)
- **br-slice-auditor**: Audits one business requirement (BR1-BR9) across all three layers — Spark query, gold or Cassandra table, FastAPI endpoint, React screen, sub-2s response — and reports which layers are done, half-done or not started. Delegate when a BR is claimed finished, before posting a gate artefact, or when deciding what the next vertical slice needs. Do not delegate for Spark code quality (use spark-reviewer) or for verifying a number (use data-fact-checker). ([Dat-TG/shop-insight](https://github.com/Dat-TG/shop-insight/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/br-slice-auditor.md))
- **br-tester**: Write or run a bounded Jest, Vitest, Playwright or screenshot test task when independent test work is useful. Choose the narrowest meaningful verification. ([Lordpluha/bitrate](https://github.com/Lordpluha/bitrate/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/br-tester.md) · ★4)
- **br-worker**: Own an explicitly requested end-to-end task or an assigned /br-auto issue. Delegate bounded stages only when useful, verify evidence and report completion or blockers. Preserve worktree and tracker ownership boundaries. ([Lordpluha/bitrate](https://github.com/Lordpluha/bitrate/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/br-worker.md) · ★4)
- **brace**: Design ticket workflows, SLA tiers, knowledge-base structures, and escalation paths for stage-appropriate support operations. ([firatcand/roster](https://github.com/firatcand/roster/blob/HEAD/agents/brain-organizer.md) · ★18)
- **brain-os-mode**: Use for any Brain OS task delegated to a subagent — reading entity state, checking active decisions, running focus/patterns/retro analysis, or proposing changes that need to honor active decisions. Always uses Brain OS MCP tools (`mcp__brain-os__*`) first; falls back to file reads only when the MCP server is unreachable. Invoke this agent when the parent task involves project state, prioritization, decisions, patterns, or wrap/retro work. ([brainOS-HQ/brain-os](https://github.com/brainOS-HQ/brain-os/blob/HEAD/templates/agents/brain-os-mode.md) · ★6)
- **brain-probe-strictness-auditor**: Audits probe modules for graceful-pass acceptance patterns and produces a per-probe findings report identifying which probes need a `_strict` counter. Use this agent for Phase 3.33 Step 4 (the strictness audit), or whenever a new probe is added to check whether it has graceful-pass acceptance that should be surfaced. ([femboy2112/toy-brain](https://github.com/femboy2112/toy-brain/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/brain-probe-strictness-auditor.md) · ★1)
- **brain-prompt-engineer**: Expert em engenharia de prompts pro Cerebro Hermes (qwen2.5:7b-instruct local via Ollama). Refina system prompts, gerencia conversational state com TTL, planeja cost guards (token budget + latencia), e valida outputs estruturados (JSON-schema-strict) consumidos por Brain.decide(). Use quando: ajustar prompt cerebro Hermes, debugar resposta qwen incoerente, planejar context window, adicionar few-shot exemplos, validar JSON output, otimizar tokens, projetar multi-turn dialogue (agent-zero/chat), ou consolidar memoria conversa entre sessoes. ([caiolea0/hermes-cloud-studio](https://github.com/caiolea0/hermes-cloud-studio/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/brain-prompt-engineer.md) · ★1)
- **brain-protocol**: Knowledge graph context agent. Dispatched to search the MindGraf brain for relevant context before responding to substantive queries. Use when the main conversation needs graph context about a person, project, decision, tool, or topic that may already exist in the brain. &lt;example&gt; Context: The user asks about the status of a project or person user: "What's happening with the auth migration?" assistant: Dispatches brain-protocol to search the knowledge graph for auth-related entities &lt;/example&gt; &lt;example&gt; Context: The user mentions a person by name in a planning context user: "Set up a meeting… ([TimCrooker/mindgraf-plugin](https://github.com/TimCrooker/mindgraf-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/brain-protocol.md))
- **brain-python**: Python FastAPI orchestrator. Planning, LLM routing, capability registry and guard, approval flow, WebSocket server. Use when code is being written in this layer. ([Eyup-58/Local-0](https://github.com/Eyup-58/Local-0/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/brain-python.md))
- **brain-query**: Query the Brain teaching graph with natural language. Translates to Cypher, synthesizes insights, never exposes raw data. ([jsagir/mindrian-os-plugin](https://github.com/jsagir/mindrian-os-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/brain-query.md) · ★1)
- **brain-reader**: My Business Brain reader. Reads the business documents it is given (contracts, price lists, policies, SOPs, reports) and returns the facts they state as a JSON list of candidate entries for the coordinator to merge. Read-only: it cannot write files, run commands or use the web. Use for bulk loads, one reader per batch of 3 to 5 documents. ([the-abraham-brand/my-business-brain](https://github.com/the-abraham-brand/my-business-brain/blob/HEAD/agents/brain-reader.md))
- **brain-ready**: The readiness check. Receives only the brief the brain would send a builder or a validator, reads the graph about its area with the brain CLI, and answers READY or NOT READY with the questions the brain must still ask the user. The brain runs it before every builder and every validator. ([Baudin999/brain](https://github.com/Baudin999/brain/blob/HEAD/harness/agents/brain-ready.md))
- **brain-researcher**: Read-only research worker for a Monkey Brain. Delegate ONE focused slice of a research question — a set of wiki pages, a codebase area, or a web topic — and it returns cited findings without writing anything. Spawn several in parallel (one per slice) for wide coverage at low context cost; the lead synthesizes and files. Used by /brain:research for fan-out. ([NeamulMorshed/The-Monkey-Brain](https://github.com/NeamulMorshed/The-Monkey-Brain/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/brain-researcher.md) · ★2)
- **brain-researcher**: Read-only researcher for the second brain and the codebase. Use to explore project_brain/ or the repo and return a compact distilled summary, keeping verbose discovery out of the main thread. Cheap (haiku), never writes. ([Xeror-Hew/AI-second-brain](https://github.com/Xeror-Hew/AI-second-brain/blob/HEAD/Claude%20Code%20Blueprint/.claude/agents/brain-researcher.md) · ★1)
- **brain-researcher**: Read-only researcher for the second brain and your files. Use to explore project_brain/ or the project files and return a compact distilled summary, keeping verbose discovery out of the main thread. Cheap (haiku), never writes. ([Xeror-Hew/AI-second-brain](https://github.com/Xeror-Hew/AI-second-brain/blob/HEAD/Claude%20Cowork%20Blueprint/.claude/agents/brain-researcher.md) · ★1)
- **brain-researcher**: My Business Brain researcher. Takes a tasking memo from the Chief of Staff and finds outside information the business needs (official rules, fees, rates, deadlines, market and competitor facts) from the most authoritative current sources, applying the brain's source tiers. Social posts and forums come back as leads, never facts. Read-only to the brain; returns a structured report. Use when the Chief of Staff routes research to it. ([the-abraham-brand/my-business-brain](https://github.com/the-abraham-brand/my-business-brain/blob/HEAD/agents/brain-researcher.md))
- **brain-reviewer**: AI-Brain 볼트의 상태를 점검하고 승격 후보·지식 갭·CPM 입력을 보고한다. 주간 정리(금요일)와 월말 리뷰에 쓴다. 아무것도 쓰지 않는 읽기 전용 에이전트. 트리거 — "볼트 상태 점검", "주간 정리 뭐 있어", "승격 후보 뽑아줘", "review the vault", "what is ready to promote". ([KIMSANGHYEON93/ai-brain-plugin](https://github.com/KIMSANGHYEON93/ai-brain-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/brain-reviewer.md))
- **brain-reviewer**: Read-only five-axis reviewer for Brain diffs. Use after any non-trivial change, before a PR merges. Never the author of the change. ([sshivanshg/brain](https://github.com/sshivanshg/brain/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/brain-reviewer.md))
- **brain-reviewer**: Validates brain consistency — checks entries against actual code, finds contradictions ([Xattaus/claude-brain](https://github.com/Xattaus/claude-brain/blob/HEAD/templates/agents/brain-reviewer.md))
- **brain-router**: Use PROACTIVELY for ambiguous or compound code-quality requests. Top-level orchestrator. Delegate here when the user asks for "full project checkup", compound commands like "clean and optimize", or ambiguous requests. Reads CLAUDE.md, classifies MD files, routes to craft-specialist or perf-specialist. ([chanjoongx/stetkeep](https://github.com/chanjoongx/stetkeep/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/brain-router.md) · 2 copies)
- **brain-router**: Agent node for `brain-router`. ([Hvuj/The-Soverign-Imperia-citadel](https://github.com/Hvuj/The-Soverign-Imperia-citadel/blob/HEAD/docs/brain/nodes/agents/brain-router.md))
- **brain-router**: Read-only graph router that selects 1-3 relevant brain nodes/files/tests/agents without loading the full graph. ([Hvuj/The-Soverign-Imperia-citadel](https://github.com/Hvuj/The-Soverign-Imperia-citadel/blob/HEAD/src/citadel/assets/claude_template/agents/brain-router.md))
- **brain-row-implementer**: Implement one or a small range of INVARIANT_CATALOG.md rows in toy-brain. Stops when the targeted rows pass `python3 -m brain.invariants run`. ([femboy2112/toy-brain](https://github.com/femboy2112/toy-brain/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/brain-row-implementer.md) · ★1)

Reads the row, the fixture, the owning module, and the cited Lean theorem; proposes the smallest change that turns the row green without breaking neighbors. - **brain-scheduler**: Distributed brain legion agent: `brain-scheduler`. ([Hvuj/The-Soverign-Imperia-citadel](https://github.com/Hvuj/The-Soverign-Imperia-citadel/blob/HEAD/docs/brain/nodes/agents/brain-scheduler.md))
- **brain-scheduler**: Distributed workflow scheduler. Decomposes non-trivial tasks into shards, dependencies, workers, and budget modes. ([Hvuj/The-Soverign-Imperia-citadel](https://github.com/Hvuj/The-Soverign-Imperia-citadel/blob/HEAD/src/citadel/assets/claude_template/agents/brain-scheduler.md))
- **brain-scout**: Hydro Brain scout (layer 6). Use for any search that spans many files, folders or vault shelves, so the director's context stays small. Give it a work order (parent_step, bring, shelves, exclude, budget). It returns a menu of 2-3 pointed options (file + line range), never a summary, and says "not found" instead of guessing. ([xelnium-dynamics/xellcell](https://github.com/xelnium-dynamics/xellcell/blob/HEAD/agents/brain-scout.md) · ★2)
- **brain-scribe**: Hydro Brain scribe (layer 10). Use at closeout to draft a session's task-summary.md and brain-summary.md from its records (contract, checkpoints, evidence.jsonl, verdict files, analysis.json), not from memory. Every claim must point to a record; anything without one is marked unverified. ([xelnium-dynamics/xellcell](https://github.com/xelnium-dynamics/xellcell/blob/HEAD/agents/brain-scribe.md) · ★2)
- **brain-search**: Read-only search of the owner's brain. Use for wide questions that need many notes ("everything about X", "what do I know about Y", "find all notes that mention Z"). Returns paths and one-line answers only. ([jellyjuices/brain-engine](https://github.com/jellyjuices/brain-engine/blob/HEAD/starter/.claude/agents/brain-search.md))

- **brain-search-router**: Graph-brain specialist agent for brain search router. ([Hvuj/The-Soverign-Imperia-citadel](https://github.com/Hvuj/The-Soverign-Imperia-citadel/blob/HEAD/src/citadel/assets/claude_template/agents/brain-search-router.md))
- **brain-spec-refresher**: Apply the SPEC_UPDATES.md refresh protocol when the upstream Lean at github.com/femboy2112/lean-scratch evolves. Runs the diff, classifies each change per the four conditions in SPEC_UPDATES.md section 4, proposes catalog edits, and re-runs the runner. - **brain-syncer**: 볼트(md)의 용어·런북을 Notion 미러 DB에 증분 동기화한다. 볼트 수와 Notion 행 수가 어긋났을 때 쓴다. 새 행 생성까지만 하고 삭제·본문 편집은 하지 않는다. 트리거 — "Notion 동기화", "볼트랑 Notion 맞춰줘", "미러 갱신", "sync notion mirror". - **brain-tracker**: Updates Brain CLAUDE.md files with decisions, status, context, and cross-brain connections. Use PROACTIVELY when significant decisions are made or documents created within a Brain. MUST BE USED for updating Brain context or tracking progress. Triggers on: "update brain", "track decision", "log this to brain", "add to CLAUDE.md", "document this decision". - **brain-updater**: Cross-project dashboard updater. Run after context-updater when project status, phase, or priority changed during the session. Updates _brain/Dashboard.md with current state from all projects. ([CAdidas333/claude-code-starter-kit](https://github.com/CAdidas333/claude-code-starter-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/brain-updater.md))
- **brain-vault-engineer**: Use for per-agent memory or the secrets vault when work touches recall/retain, namespaces, embeddings, envelope encryption, grants, or read audit. - **brain-verifier**: Hydro Brain verifier (layer 8, before the judge). Use before step 6 on standard and deep work, and whenever a summary or report is about to go to the owner. Give it the session id (or the summary file) and the artifact paths. It checks every claim against the files, the evidence log and the Tier-0 results, runs nothing that changes files, and returns the claims it could not support. - **brain-verifier**: Notion 미러와 볼트의 무결성을 검증한다. 중복 행, 한글 낱자 손상으로 생긴 유령 중복, 고아 행, 깨진 링크를 찾아 격리 페이지로 이동을 제안·실행한다. 동기화 직후와 주간 정리에 쓴다. 영구 삭제는 하지 않는다. 트리거 — "Notion 중복 확인", "미러 검증", "행 수가 안 맞아", "verify the notion mirror". ([KIMSANGHYEON93/ai-brain-plugin](https://github.com/KIMSANGHYEON93/ai-brain-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/brain-verifier.md))
- **brain-worker**: Hydro Brain worker (layer 6). Use for routine, well-specified edits or commands that the director has already decided (rename across files, apply a listed set of changes, run a checklist), so the director keeps its context for hard parts. Needs an exact work order; reports every file changed and every check it ran. ([xelnium-dynamics/xellcell](https://github.com/xelnium-dynamics/xellcell/blob/HEAD/agents/brain-worker.md) · ★2)
- **brain-worker**: Read-only BrainLayer lookups and claim checks; return compact source citations to the caller. Never stores. ([EtanHey/golems](https://github.com/EtanHey/golems/blob/HEAD/templates/gemini/agents/brain-worker.md))
- **brain-worker**: Off-grid BrainLayer recon sub-agent. Dispatched by a parent (orc, a domain lead, or a cmux worker) with a highly specific question. Never stores. - **brain-writer**: Records facts, decisions, rejected options and service contracts in the vault. ([odinokov/lean-harness](https://github.com/odinokov/lean-harness/blob/HEAD/assets/agents/brain-writer.md))
- **brain1-maintainer**: Cheap maintenance subagent for the Brain1 Obsidian vault — runs /graphify --update, lint passes, refresh-queue scans, and status-hygiene checks. Do NOT dispatch for tasks requiring synthesis, contradiction-resolution, page restructuring, or any judgment call — those belong in the parent session. - **brain_storm**: 論理推論・背景理解・多角的アプローチで多様なアイデアを生成する。 ([nak4mura/config_files](https://github.com/nak4mura/config_files/blob/HEAD/opencode/agents/brain_storm.md))

- **brainds-connection-mapper**: Connection-mapping executor. - **brainds-currency-elicitor**: Brick-E currency elicitation sub-agent. - **brainds-kpi-composer**: Standalone on-demand KPI dossier composer. - **brainds-source-explorer**: Read-only data source exploration agent. - **brainstorm**: Use when the user has a net-new software project idea that needs shaping into a brief before tasks can be created. - **brainstorm**: 脑力激荡官，负责与主 agent 进行深度讨论与争辩，挑战假设、提出替代方案，以产出最优设计方案。Use when user or another agent wants to brainstorm, debate a design decision, explore alternatives, or stress-test a plan. ([ai-pivot/xbot](https://github.com/ai-pivot/xbot/blob/HEAD/.xbot.example/agents/brainstorm.md) · ★15)
- **brainstorm**: Use this agent to decide the best next direction for a Prism space (product/project) and turn that decision into new Kanban tasks. - **brainstorm**: You are **Brainstorm**, a product brief agent. - **branch-ff**: Fast-forward lane. Loops on an interval and keeps the PRIMARY checkout's integration branch at the remote tip with a safe `git pull --ff-only` — so every worktree an agent cuts starts from a current base (hooks, scripts, skills). Purely mechanical — run the tick, match the decision table, report one line. When the fast-forward is refused (local changes would be overwritten, or the branch diverged) it ALERTS and changes nothing — never stashes, resets, rebases, or merges. Never touches worktrees. Singleton. Run with "/loop &lt;interval&gt; start". ([skyfox675/agents-skills](https://github.com/skyfox675/agents-skills/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/branch-ff.md) · ★11 · 7 copies)

- **branch-protection**: - GitHub Actions `quality` 配置为单个静态检查 job；行为测试由本地 Mac 的受影响范围验证或显式完整入口承担。 - 历史 CI 的成功、失败单测、伪造密钥和被跟踪数据库阻断已通过独立 PR 验证；该历史结果不证明当前改动的行为验收。 - 截至 2026-08-22，当前 GitHub 方案不支持私有仓库的 branch protection 或 rulesets；两个官方 API 均返回 HTTP 403，要求升级 GitHub Pro 或将仓库公开。 - 仓库所有者决定继续保持私有，不为分支保护升级方案，也不再配置服务端 branch protection 或 rulesets。 - `main` 没有平台强制保护；以下规则由仓库所有者在本地和 PR 流程中维护，不得表述为 GitHub 已强制执行。 ([CleverC2200/oa-workspace-minimal](https://github.com/CleverC2200/oa-workspace-minimal/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/branch-protection.md))
- **branch-protection**: What protects `master`, how to read it back, and what the rules mean for you. The rules live on GitHub, not in this repository, so this is the reference for them. - **branch-protection**: Owned by `security`. Applying or changing protection on `main` is a pass gate: security prepares the command, the user runs it. ([DerekHertz/DimSumDev](https://github.com/DerekHertz/DimSumDev/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/branch-protection.md))

- **branch-protection-audit**: Audits branch protection configuration. Use when checking if a repo enforces required reviews, status checks, and prevents direct pushes to main. ([aboavent/payments-demo](https://github.com/aboavent/payments-demo/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/branch-protection-audit.md))
